Excluded upstream components (not implemented controls):
- MSFT Internet Explorer 11 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows 11 24H2 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows 11 24H2 - Credential Guard: Requires virtualization-based security support in the virtual hardware (Secure Boot, TPM, virtualization extensions) and a reboot; not applied automatically. Configure it through Group Policy after deployment on capable hosts.
- registry:HKLM\SOFTWARE\Policies\Microsoft\Windows\DeviceInstall\Restrictions\DenyDeviceClasses\**delvals.: list-replacement marker (delete all other values in the key); the values the GPO defines in this key are enforced individually, but pre-existing additional values are not removed

Excluded upstream components (not implemented controls):
- MSFT Internet Explorer 11 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows 11 25H2 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows 11 25H2 - Credential Guard: Requires virtualization-based security support in the virtual hardware (Secure Boot, TPM, virtualization extensions) and a reboot; not applied automatically. Configure it through Group Policy after deployment on capable hosts.
- registry:HKLM\SOFTWARE\Policies\Microsoft\Windows\DeviceInstall\Restrictions\DenyDeviceClasses\**delvals.: list-replacement marker (delete all other values in the key); the values the GPO defines in this key are enforced individually, but pre-existing additional values are not removed

Excluded upstream components (not implemented controls):
- MSFT Internet Explorer 11 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows 11 26H2 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows 11 26H2 - Credential Guard: Requires virtualization-based security support in the virtual hardware (Secure Boot, TPM, virtualization extensions) and a reboot; not applied automatically. Configure it through Group Policy after deployment on capable hosts.
- registry:HKLM\SOFTWARE\Policies\Microsoft\Windows\DeviceInstall\Restrictions\DenyDeviceClasses\**delvals.: list-replacement marker (delete all other values in the key); the values the GPO defines in this key are enforced individually, but pre-existing additional values are not removed

Excluded upstream components (not implemented controls):
- MSFT Internet Explorer 11 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows 10 1809 and Server 2019 Member Server - Credential Guard: Requires virtualization-based security support in the virtual hardware (Secure Boot, TPM, virtualization extensions) and a reboot; not applied automatically. Configure it through Group Policy after deployment on capable hosts.

Excluded upstream components (not implemented controls):
- MSFT Internet Explorer 11 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows Server 2022 - Member Server Credential Guard: Requires virtualization-based security support in the virtual hardware (Secure Boot, TPM, virtualization extensions) and a reboot; not applied automatically. Configure it through Group Policy after deployment on capable hosts.
- MSFT Windows Server 2022 - Domain Controller: Domain controllers are not supported.
- MSFT Windows Server 2022 - Domain Controller Virtualization Based Security: Domain controllers are not supported.

Excluded upstream components (not implemented controls):
- MSFT Internet Explorer 11 - User: User Configuration policy; this provisioner configures computer policy only.
- MSFT Windows Server 2025 v2602 - Member Server Credential Guard: Requires virtualization-based security support in the virtual hardware (Secure Boot, TPM, virtualization extensions) and a reboot; not applied automatically. Configure it through Group Policy after deployment on capable hosts.
- MSFT Windows Server 2025 v2602 - Domain Controller: Domain controllers are not supported.
- MSFT Windows Server 2025 v2602 - Domain Controller Virtualization Based Security: Domain controllers are not supported.
- registry:HKLM\Software\Policies\Microsoft\Windows NT\DNSClient\DnsPolicyConfig: key-only marker in the GPO (REG_NONE without a value name); there is no value to enforce
